## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如，你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you keep chatting.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 말씀드리면, 예를 들어 "이 프로젝트에서 실패하는 테스트를 찾아서 고쳐줘"처럼 말씀해 주시면 작업을 맡아서 끝나면 알려드릴게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 네, 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 봐요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCP は届いたことと順番を保証し、UDP は保証しない代わりに軽くて速いのが一番の違いです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, y merge para integrar una rama que ya está compartida, porque rebase reescribe la historia y no debes hacerlo sobre commits que otros ya tienen. Regla simple: si nadie más ha bajado esa rama, rebase; si otros la usan, merge.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours alone and not pushed or shared yet, to tidy its commits onto the latest base before review.
Merge when others already work on the branch or you've published it, since rewriting shared history breaks their copies.

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用路径直接指定文件就行：

```bash
bun test src/utils/parse.test.ts
```

`bun test` 后面的参数会当作过滤条件，匹配文件路径，所以写文件名的一部分也能跑到：

```bash
bun test parse
```

如果只想跑某个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test -t "返回空数组"
```

两个可以组合使用，比如只跑某文件里的某个用例：

```bash
bun test parse.test.ts -t "返回空数组"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where a database first records every change it's about to make, before touching the main data. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart to recover the changes that were committed and roll back the ones that weren't.
```

### past-compaction-pr-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点
  [partner ack line] 好，我接着改 compaction 那个 PR 的锁粒度，改完告诉你。
  [sent to task compaction_pr] 用户原话："接着上周那个 compaction 的 PR，把 review 里说的锁粒度改细一点"

要做的：
- 找到这个 PR 的 review 里关于锁粒度的意见，按它说的把锁改细（缩小临界区 / 拆成更细的锁，按 review 的具体建议来）。
- 改动只限于 review 提到的锁相关部分，不顺手改别的。
- 跑相关测试和 compaction 的已有测试，确认没有回归；如果有并发相关的测试，也跑一遍看有没有死锁或竞争。

完成标准（报告里要给出证据）：
- 改了哪些地方、各自原来锁在哪、现在锁在哪（一两句话即可）。
- 测试命令和结果。
- 如果 review 的意见不止一条，逐条说明做没做、为什么。

不在范围内：
- 不推送到远端，不改 PR 的草稿状态，不合并。改动留在它自己的分支上提交就行。
- 不改 compaction 以外的功能。

地方：就用这个任务原来的仓库和
```

  function render(){
    var f = ducter.get('fares') || null;
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
    var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    if(!f || !f.trains || !f.trains.length){
      list.innerHTML = '';
      cnt.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没查</span>';
      empty.hidden = false;
      return;
    }
    var limit = f.limit || 600;
    var under = f.trains.filter(function(t){return t.price < limit;});
    cnt.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    sub.textContent = (f.date || '周五 北京 → 上海') + (f.updated ? ' · ' + f.updated : '');
    empty.hidden = true;
    list.innerHTML = f.trains.map(function(t){
      var cls = t.price < limit ? 'ok' : 'over';
      return '<div class="k-row ' + cls + '"><div><div class="k-row-main">' + esc(t.no) + '</div>' +
        (t.seats ? '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(t.seats) + '</div>' : '') +
        '</div><div class="k-row-value">¥' + esc(t.price) + '</div></div>';
    }).join('');
    ducter.summary(under.length + ' 班在 ' + limit + ' 元以下');
  }
  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"fares":{"date":"周五 北京 → 上海","updated":"第 3 天","limit":600,"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 12 张"},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":"二等座余 4 张"},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":""}]}}

<section class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest writes/s</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="w"><span class="k-none">Not yet</span></div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="s">p99 latency and compaction stalls show here</div>
</section>
<h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
<div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div></div>
<div class="k-empty" id="empty">Nothing yet. Each night's run adds a row.</div>
<script>
function render() {
  var nights = ducter.get('nights') || [];
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  list.textContent = '';
  empty.style.display = nights.length ? 'none' : 'block';
  nights.forEach(function (n) {
    var row = document.createElement('div');
    row.className = 'k-row';
    var main = document.createElement('div');
    main.className = 'k-row-main';
    main.textContent = n.night;
    var sub = document.createElement('div');
    sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
    sub.textContent = 'p99 ' + n.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + n.stalls;
    var val = document.createElement('div');
    val.className = 'k-row-value';
    val.textContent = n.writes + ' writes/s';
    main.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(main);
    row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  });
  if (nights.length) {
    var last = nights[nights.length - 1];
    document.getElementById('w').textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
    document.getElementById('s').textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls;
    ducter.summary(last.night + ': ' + last.writes + ' writes/s');
  }
}
ducter.onChange(render);
render();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":0},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":0}]}

}

  var id = e.target.getAttribute("data-id");
  if (!id) return;

  render();
});
if (typeof ducter !== "undefined" && ducter.get) {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function(v){ if (v) landed = v; render(); });
  if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function(){ Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function(v){ landed = v || {}; render(); }); });
} else { render(); }

<div class="k-field"><label>金额</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
<button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button>

</div></details>
</main>
<script>
const K … ML=xs.map((x,i)=>'<div class="k-row" data-i="'+i+'"><div class="k-row-main">'+esc(x.trip)+'</div><div class="k-row-sub">'+esc(x.day)+' '+esc(x.cat)+'</div><div class="k-row-value">'+fmt(x.amt)+'</div></div>').join("");

document.querySelectorAll(".k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>setCat(b.dataset.cat));
$("list").onclick=e=>{
const r=e.target.closest(".k-row");if(!r)return;
const x=(ducter.get(KEY)||[])[+r.dataset.i];if(!x)return;
editing=+r.dataset.i;$("trip").value=x.trip||"";$("day").value=x.day||"";$("amt").value=x.amt;setCat(x.cat||"交通");
$("del").hidden=false;document.querySelector(".k-add").open=true;
};
$("save").onclick=()=>{
const a=Number($("amt").value),t=$("trip").value.trim();
if(!t||!a)return;
const xs=ducter.get(KEY)||[];
const item={trip:t,day:$("day").value.trim(),cat:cat,amt:a};
if(editing>=0&&editing<xs.length)xs[editing]=item;else xs.push(item);
ducter.set(KEY,xs);reset();draw();
};
$("del").onclick=()=>{
const xs=ducter.get(KEY)||[];
if(editing>=0&&editing<xs.length){xs.splice(editing,1);ducter.set(KEY,xs)}
reset();draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差 · 亚朵酒店（望京）","day":"11/20–11/21","cat":"住宿","amt":1036}]}

```

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一台3D风格的米色笔记本电脑，屏幕是空白的，边框是深藏青色，键盘和触控板都画得很清楚。整体柔和、低饱和，看起来像一个温和的小插图。

适合放在空状态里。它不吵，屏幕空着正好对应"还没有内容"，也容易让人联想到"可以开始写点什么"。尺寸320×246，显示在120到160像素宽的位置上应该够清楚。

要注意两点：一是它是3D渲染风格，如果你的界面是扁平线条风，放进去会不太统一；二是空白屏幕本身不会说话，最好配一句提示文字，比如"还没有记录，点这里新建一条"。
```

  [sent to task ttl] 用户的原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

请在现有的 TTL 测试（已改为假时钟）基础上，再补一条测试：把时钟推进到恰好等于过期时间的那一刻，断言此时该条目已经视为过期（取不到值）；并在它之前一个时间单位（例如到期前 1 毫秒或 1 个 tick）断言仍然有效。这样边界两侧都覆盖。

完成标准：新测试通过；整个测试文件连跑 20 次全部通过；把跑测试的输出作为证据汇报。

不在范围内：不要改动已有的 values() 相关代码和测试；不要合并到主分支、不要推送、不要开 PR。改动仍只在当前任务的分支上提交。

地点：~/dev/foo 仓库，沿用本任务的分支。
```
